bg-neutral-90"><p class="text-web3-18-heading lg:text-web3-20 font-body">Key takeaways</p><div class="mt-4 [&amp;_p_a]:!inline portable-text-breaks"><ul class="my-4 md:w-10/12 [&amp;&gt;li]:mb-2.5 web3-custom-bullet"><li><strong>State-of-the-art retrieval</strong>: Rerank 5 Pro leads on major public benchmarks, while Rerank 5 Fast delivers near-leading performance at lower cost.</li><li><strong>Strong across enterprise domains</strong>: Rerank 5 establishes itself as a leader across finance, healthcare, scientific research, parsed documents and multilingual retrieval.</li><li><strong>Context Focus</strong>: new to Rerank 5 - a single Rerank call returns the most relevant passages within each document, cutting LLM token costs with minimal loss in answer quality.</li><li><strong>Calibrated relevance scores</strong>: consistent scores across queries and domains let teams set one threshold to decide what reaches the LLM.</li><li><strong>Deploy anywhere</strong>: available through the Cohere API, Model Vault, Microsoft Foundry, Amazon SageMaker, privately in your own VPC or on-premises, and to all North users. Get started with just a few lines of code.</li></ul></div></div><div class="lg:[&amp;&gt;*:not(figure):not(blockquote):not(.portable-in-content-banner)]:max-w-[670px] lg:[&amp;&gt;.portable-marketo-form]:my-[48px] blog-post-body mt-7 lg:mt-12 [&amp;&gt;div.content-disruptor]:my-10 lg:[&amp;&gt;div.content-disruptor]:my-20 [&amp;_p_a]:!inline portable-text-breaks"><h2 class="text-web3-20 lg:text-web3-24 font-body">Introduction</h2><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">Today, we&#x27;re releasing Rerank 5, our most advanced enterprise retrieval models yet, available in Pro and Fast tiers.<br/><br/><a href="http://www.cohere.com/rerank" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">Rerank 5</span></a> helps teams find the right information, send the most relevant context to their LLMs, and give customers more tailored search experiences. With Rerank 5 Pro, we’ve set a new standard in reranking quality, especially on reasoning-intensive search, visually rich documents, and key enterprise domains. Rerank 5 Fast brings that quality to latency- and cost-sensitive workloads. Both models introduce Context Focus, which pinpoints the most relevant spans of text within documents, going beyond document- or chunk-level retrieval to surface precisely the information that matters.<br/><br/>Every token your LLM reads is a token you pay for, and retrieval decides which tokens those are. As agents run more searches over longer documents, retrieval has become a key lever enterprises have over their AI cost, latency, and answer quality.<br/><br/>Rerank 5 joins <a href="https://cohere.com/blog/embed-5" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">Embed 5</span></a> and <a href="https://cohere.com/blog/parse" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">Parse 5</span></a> as part of our latest generation of search models. It is generally available today on the <a href="http://dashboard.cohere.com" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">Cohere API</span></a> and <a href="https://vault.cohere.com/" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">Model Vault</span></a>, <a href="https://ai.azure.com/catalog/models/Cohere-rerank-v5-pro" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">Microsoft Foundry</span></a>, <a href="https://aws.amazon.com/marketplace/pp/prodview-6hcingojlkj32" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">Amazon SageMaker</span></a>, and to all <a href="http://cohere.com/north" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">North</span></a> users. API pricing is $0.05 per 1M tokens for Pro and $0.02 for Fast.</p><h2 class="text-web3-20 lg:text-web3-24 font-body">Performance</h2><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">Rerank 5 Pro leads public benchmarks such as <a href="https://huggingface.co/blog/QuentinJG/introducing-vidore-v3" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">ViDoRe V3</span></a>, <a href="https://huggingface.co/collections/zeta-alpha-ai/nanobeir" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">nanoMTEB</span></a>, <a href="https://brightbenchmark.github.io/" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">BRIGHT</span></a>, and <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2104.08663" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">BEIR</span></a> - outperforming every competing model tested. Of the 46 composite datasets evaluated, Rerank 5 Pro ranked first on 39 and placed at least second in 43. Models are evaluated using <a href="http://www.cohere.com/blog/rcp-ndcg" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">RCP-nDCG@10</span></a>, a Cohere-developed methodology designed to address limitations in traditional nDCG caused by incomplete relevance judgments (qrel coverage).<br/><br/>The advantage is particularly pronounced on BRIGHT, a benchmark testing reasoning-intensive retrieval, where identifying relevant documents requires deeper understanding beyond keyword or semantic similarity. Rerank 5 Pro scores 82.2, outperforming Voyage AI Rerank 3 by 5.8 points and Rerank 4 Pro by 12.4 points, its largest generational gain across the four headline benchmarks. Pro also leads the strongest external competitor by 1.6 points on ViDoRe V3, 1.8 on BEIR, and 3.4 on nanoMTEB. These gains are increasingly important for agentic workflows, where complex, multi-step queries demand the ability to reason about relevance and return evidence that may not closely resemble the original query.<br/><br/>Rerank 5 Fast delivers substantial quality gains in the speed-optimized tier. In all four benchmarks, it outperforms Voyage AI Rerank 3 Lite and Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B, as well as the much larger Qwen3-Reranker-8B and previous-generation Rerank 4 Pro.</p><figure class="flex h-full flex-col justify-center items-center"><img alt="" class="w-full rounded-lg" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 800px" srcSet="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/f7a7f8d8fe71a73238450f2638d6d7d0ad896f7b-3140x1412.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=200 200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/f7a7f8d8fe71a73238450f2638d6d7d0ad896f7b-3140x1412.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=400 400w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/f7a7f8d8fe71a73238450f2638d6d7d0ad896f7b-3140x1412.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=600 600w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/f7a7f8d8fe71a73238450f2638d6d7d0ad896f7b-3140x1412.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800 800w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/f7a7f8d8fe71a73238450f2638d6d7d0ad896f7b-3140x1412.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1200 1200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/f7a7f8d8fe71a73238450f2638d6d7d0ad896f7b-3140x1412.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1600 1600w" src="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/f7a7f8d8fe71a73238450f2638d6d7d0ad896f7b-3140x1412.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800" width="800" height="360"/><figcaption class="mt-3 font-body text-web3-12"><div class="[&amp;_p_a]:!inline portable-text-breaks"><span class="">Headline benchmarks. Reranking quality (RCP-nDCG@10) on four public benchmarks: ViDoRe V3 (8 datasets), BEIR (13), BRIGHT (12) and nanoMTEB (13). Scores are averaged across each benchmark&#x27;s datasets.</span></div></figcaption></figure><h3 class="text-web3-18-heading lg:text-web3-20 font-body">Domain-specific performance</h3><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">Across specialized enterprise domains, Rerank 5 demonstrates consistent leadership. In finance and economics, Rerank 5 Pro leads competition on visual retrieval from financial documents (ViDoRe Finance), numerical reasoning over financial reports (<a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.00122" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">FinQA</span></a>), and complex economics questions (BRIGHT Economics). This is where its lead is largest, outperforming Voyage AI Rerank 3 by 9.6 points.<br/><br/>Similar results extend across healthcare, scientific research, and energy-focused retrieval, where benchmarks test the ability to surface relevant information from specialized scientific literature, biomedical research, and climate-related content. See the full list of results <a href="https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1FYXax6vFdKE4ph3eXj935jCl7mpeczvYe5jye2h1f9U/edit?gid=0#gid=0" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap datasets.</span></div></figcaption></figure><h3 class="text-web3-18-heading lg:text-web3-20 font-body">Multilingual</h3><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">Rerank 5 Pro improves on Rerank 4 Pro in every language we evaluated, with gains of at least two points in Spanish, French, German, Arabic and Korean. It ranks first on 14 of the 19 non-English languages in our internal evaluation suite, including all six of the priority business languages shown below.<br/><br/>Against the competition, Pro leads Voyage AI Rerank 3 in every one of these languages. Its widest margins are in Japanese (+2.7), French (+2.3) and Arabic (+2.3). The entire suite consists of 20 world languages and 74 datasets.</p><h3 class="text-web3-18-heading lg:text-web3-20 font-body">Parsed PDFs</h3><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">On retrieval tasks involving content extracted from PDFs, an important capability for enterprise document search, Pro achieves the highest average score of 89.9 in the evaluated suite. This puts it ahead of Voyage AI Rerank 3 (88.4) and Rerank 4 Pro (88.3). Fast follows closely at 88.5, marginally outperforming both models.</p><figure class="flex h-full flex-col justify-center items-center"><img alt="" class="w-full rounded-lg" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 800px" srcSet="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/7531120bb5a7e6aec051200db15e424019fb2e9a-3140x1340.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=200 200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/7531120bb5a7e6aec051200db15e424019fb2e9a-3140x1340.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=400 400w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/7531120bb5a7e6aec051200db15e424019fb2e9a-3140x1340.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=600 600w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/7531120bb5a7e6aec051200db15e424019fb2e9a-3140x1340.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800 800w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/7531120bb5a7e6aec051200db15e424019fb2e9a-3140x1340.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1200 1200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/7531120bb5a7e6aec051200db15e424019fb2e9a-3140x1340.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1600 1600w" src="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/7531120bb5a7e6aec051200db15e424019fb2e9a-3140x1340.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800" width="800" height="341"/><figcaption class="mt-3 font-body text-web3-12"><div class="[&amp;_p_a]:!inline portable-text-breaks"><span class="">Parsed PDFs. Reranking quality (RCP-nDCG@10), averaged across 47 datasets of text parsed from PDFs. Documents were parsed using Gemini 1.5 Flash.</span></div></figcaption></figure><h3 class="text-web3-18-heading lg:text-web3-20 font-body">Latency + Throughput</h3><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">Rerank 5 Fast is built for volume. Under sustained load, Fast reranks about 187 standard-length documents (1,000 tokens) per second on an NVIDIA A100 GPU - delivering 2.9x the throughput of Pro.<br/><br/>Fast also delivers nearly 3x faster response times than Pro, making it ideal for latency-sensitive applications and agents that run many searches per task. Pro remains best suited for applications where ranking quality takes priority over speed.</p><figure class="flex h-full flex-col justify-center items-center"><img alt="" class="w-full rounded-lg" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 800px" srcSet="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/12e5663f51a63151e2cbe72511f89752a1d0b33d-3140x1572.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=200 200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/12e5663f51a63151e2cbe72511f89752a1d0b33d-3140x1572.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=400 400w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/12e5663f51a63151e2cbe72511f89752a1d0b33d-3140x1572.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=600 600w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/12e5663f51a63151e2cbe72511f89752a1d0b33d-3140x1572.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800 800w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/12e5663f51a63151e2cbe72511f89752a1d0b33d-3140x1572.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1200 1200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/12e5663f51a63151e2cbe72511f89752a1d0b33d-3140x1572.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1600 1600w" src="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/12e5663f51a63151e2cbe72511f89752a1d0b33d-3140x1572.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800" width="800" height="401"/><figcaption class="mt-3 font-body text-web3-12"><div class="[&amp;_p_a]:!inline portable-text-breaks"><span class="">Document throughput and median request latency for Rerank 5 Fast and Pro on a single NVIDIA A100 Tensor Core GPU via the Cohere API, with 96 documents per request at a concurrency of 8, over 1,000 requests per document length. Document lengths are approximate.</span></div></figcaption></figure><h2 class="text-web3-20 lg:text-web3-24 font-body">Context Focus: unlock smarter token economics</h2><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">As documents grow longer and AI agents retrieve information from more sources, the amount of context passed to LLMs continues to increase. Yet, often only a few sentences within each document are relevant to the query. Processing unnecessary content drives up token costs, increases latency and can introduce noise that reduces answer quality.<br/><br/>Context Focus, a new optional capability launching with Rerank 5, goes beyond identifying relevant documents to pinpoint the specific passages that matter most. Configurable thresholds let developers balance context reduction with evidence retention, while a single Rerank API call returns both document-level relevance scores and scored text spans. Applications can then pass only the most relevant evidence to an LLM, without adding steps to the retrieval pipeline.</p><h3 class="text-web3-18-heading lg:text-web3-20 font-body">Decouple context from answer quality</h3><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">We measured how answer quality varied as Context Focus removes more of the retrieved text across 30 question-answering datasets. Removing half the context sacrificed only 2 percentage points of answer quality (96% retained), and removing three-quarters lost just 7 (87% answer quality retained). Context Focus therefore works like a dial: moderate settings are close to free, and teams that need bigger savings can trade some quality for up to 10x less context.</p><figure class="flex h-full flex-col justify-center items-center"><img alt="" class="w-full rounded-lg" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 800px" srcSet="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/c4824f5b560c16f076719e632bdd508f35076982-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=200 200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/c4824f5b560c16f076719e632bdd508f35076982-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=400 400w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/c4824f5b560c16f076719e632bdd508f35076982-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=600 600w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/c4824f5b560c16f076719e632bdd508f35076982-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800 800w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/c4824f5b560c16f076719e632bdd508f35076982-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1200 1200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/c4824f5b560c16f076719e632bdd508f35076982-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1600 1600w" src="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/c4824f5b560c16f076719e632bdd508f35076982-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800" width="800" height="446"/><figcaption class="mt-3 font-body text-web3-12"><div class="[&amp;_p_a]:!inline portable-text-breaks"><span class="">Answer quality as retrieved text is removed. Each model picks its top 5 documents from the same pool of 50 candidates, then removes text at a range of thresholds (one dot per threshold): Rerank 5 with Context Focus, the baselines with their own compressors. Qwen3.5-9B answers from what remains, and Qwen3.5-35B grades the answer. x-axis: share of retrieved tokens removed; y-axis: average judge score; both averaged over 30 single-turn QA datasets from ViDoRe, UNICORN, BRIGHT-Pro and agentic QA (up to 500 queries each).</span></div></figcaption></figure><p class="text-web3-16 font-body"><br/>We then gave a search agent a retrieval stack built up one step at a time, starting from keyword search (BM25). Adding Rerank 5 Pro raised accuracy from 46% to 49%. Turning on Context Focus then cut tokens and raised accuracy further. At the default threshold (0.1), tokens per task fell by 19% and accuracy rose to 57%. At 0.4, tokens fell by 34%, lower on every dataset, and accuracy reached 58%. Altogether, the full stack with Context Focus at 0.4 used 35% fewer tokens than BM25 alone, at higher accuracy (58% vs 46%). On the six deep-search benchmarks, it used 41% fewer tokens, and accuracy rose from 39% to 52%, with every benchmark improving on both measures. ​</p><figure class="flex h-full flex-col justify-center items-center"><img alt="" class="w-full rounded-lg" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 800px" srcSet="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/d8f99537c9da84dd6f1417c195908d82bf65a056-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=200 200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/d8f99537c9da84dd6f1417c195908d82bf65a056-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=400 400w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/d8f99537c9da84dd6f1417c195908d82bf65a056-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=600 600w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/d8f99537c9da84dd6f1417c195908d82bf65a056-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800 800w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/d8f99537c9da84dd6f1417c195908d82bf65a056-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1200 1200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/d8f99537c9da84dd6f1417c195908d82bf65a056-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1600 1600w" src="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/d8f99537c9da84dd6f1417c195908d82bf65a056-3140x1752.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800" width="800" height="446"/><figcaption class="mt-3 font-body text-web3-12"><div class="[&amp;_p_a]:!inline portable-text-breaks"><span class="">Search-agent results, averaged over 17 datasets including six deep-search benchmarks (BrowseComp-Plus, MedBrowseComp, WebWalkerQA, SealQA, HERB and DRBench), with 50 questions per dataset. We follow the evaluation setup of BrowseComp-Plus. The agent is glm-5-3-flash, with search as its only tool and 5 results per search. Retrieval starts with BM25, and Rerank 5 Pro reorders the top 100 with Context Focus switched off. Context Focus is then shown at the default threshold (0.1) and at 0.4, each applied on top of Rerank 5 Pro. Accuracy is the percentage of questions answered correctly. Tokens are the average total per task, summed over every LLM call in the run: input, output and reasoning tokens. The prompt for each call includes the full conversation so far, and cached tokens are included.</span></div></figcaption></figure><h2 class="text-web3-20 lg:text-web3-24 font-body">Calibrated relevance scores</h2><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">With Rerank 5, a score of 0.7 now means a 70% chance the document is useful.<br/><br/>Every reranker returns a relevance score, but until now those scores were mostly good for sorting. What counted as a &quot;good&quot; score changed from model to model. Teams had to tune a cutoff for each model and re-tune it on every upgrade.<br/><br/>Rerank 5&#x27;s scores are calibrated out of the box: the <code class="rounded p-0.5 font-code text-web3-14 leading-[inherit] bg-gray-200">relevance_score</code> returned with each result reads as the probability that the document is useful, with no change to your request or the response format. Pro and Fast also agree on where the line is: &quot;more likely useful than not&quot; is a score of 0.50 for Pro and 0.45 for Fast.</p><figure class="flex h-full flex-col justify-center items-center"><img alt="" class="w-full rounded-lg" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 800px" srcSet="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/329185849ba3b29f2166a4e12420cb9b47d1407f-3140x1840.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=200 200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/329185849ba3b29f2166a4e12420cb9b47d1407f-3140x1840.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=400 400w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/329185849ba3b29f2166a4e12420cb9b47d1407f-3140x1840.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=600 600w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/329185849ba3b29f2166a4e12420cb9b47d1407f-3140x1840.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800 800w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/329185849ba3b29f2166a4e12420cb9b47d1407f-3140x1840.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1200 1200w, https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/329185849ba3b29f2166a4e12420cb9b47d1407f-3140x1840.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=1600 1600w" src="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rjtqmwfu/web3-prod/329185849ba3b29f2166a4e12420cb9b47d1407f-3140x1840.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;q=80&amp;w=800" width="800" height="469"/><figcaption class="mt-3 font-body text-web3-12"><div class="[&amp;_p_a]:!inline portable-text-breaks"><span class="">Rerank 5&#x27;s raw scores read as probabilities. Documents are sorted into ten bins by score, and each line connects a model&#x27;s bins: the bin&#x27;s average score (across) against the share of its documents judged useful (up). On the dotted diagonal, a score is exactly a probability; lines below it overstate relevance, and lines above it understate it. Scores are used as returned, with no recalibration. Left: blind human graders, 2,262 documents; useful = a mean grade of &quot;Useful&quot; or higher on a five-point scale (Off-topic, Topical, Useful, Answers, Complete). Right: NIST TREC-DL assessors, 20,646 documents; useful = grade 2 (&quot;highly relevant&quot;) or higher on NIST&#x27;s 0–3 scale.</span></div></figcaption></figure><p class="text-web3-16 font-body"><br/>We checked this against two independent sets of human judgments that no model was trained on: a blind panel of graders and NIST&#x27;s TREC Deep Learning assessors. On both, Rerank 5 follows the diagonal far more closely than any other reranker. Competing models sit below it, overstating relevance: documents Voyage AI Rerank 3 scored around 0.55 were only 24% useful to the human graders.<br/><br/>The table below sums this up as expected calibration error (ECE): the average gap between a model&#x27;s scores and the real share of useful documents, where 0 is perfect. Rerank 5&#x27;s is about 0.06 on both sets of judgments; every other reranker we tested has nearly four times that error or more. Calibration doesn&#x27;t cost ranking quality, either: Rerank 5 Pro and Fast rank first and second in average precision on both.</p><div><style>

</div></div><p class="text-web3-12 font-body"><br/>Expected Calibration Error sums up each line in the charts above: the average distance of its dots from the diagonal, weighted by how many documents each dot represents. In other words, how far a model&#x27;s scores are from the real share of useful documents. Lower is better; 0 is perfect.</p><h3 class="text-web3-18-heading lg:text-web3-20 font-body">Why it matters</h3><ul class="my-4 md:w-10/12 [&amp;&gt;li]:mb-2.5 web3-custom-bullet"><li><strong>Filter by relevance, not by count.</strong> Instead of passing a fixed top-k to your LLM, keep every document above the threshold. Narrow queries pass a few documents, broad ones pass more, and the model sees less noise.</li><li><strong>Know when nothing relevant came back.</strong> If no document clears the threshold, an agent can rewrite the query or say it doesn&#x27;t know, instead of answering from whatever ranked first.</li><li><strong>One threshold across domains and model sizes.</strong> Across BRIGHT, NanoBEIR, and ViDoRe V3, the default stays between 0.47 and 0.53 for Pro, and Pro and Fast are within 0.05 of each other.</li></ul><h2 class="text-web3-20 lg:text-web3-24 font-body">Start reranking</h2><p </span><span>    </span><span style="color:#39594D">print</span><span>(</span><span style="color:#CA492D">f&quot;</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">{result[</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">&#x27;relevance_score&#x27;</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">]:</span><span style="color:#39594D">.2</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">f}</span><span style="color:#CA492D">  </span><span style="color:#9B60AA">{document[:</span><span style="color:#39594D">60</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">]}</span><span style="color:#CA492D">...&quot;</span><span>)
</span><span>    </span><span style="color:#2D4CB9">for</span><span> span </span><span style="color:#2D4CB9">in</span><span> result.get(</span><span style="color:#CA492D">&quot;relevant_spans&quot;</span><span>) </span><span style="color:#2D4CB9">or</span><span> []:
</span><span>        </span><span style="color:#39594D">print</span><span>(</span><span style="color:#CA492D">f&quot;      </span><span style="color:#9B60AA">{span[</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">&#x27;score&#x27;</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">]:</span><span style="color:#39594D">.2</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">f}</span><span style="color:#CA492D">  </span><span style="color:#9B60AA">{document[span[</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">&#x27;start&#x27;</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">]:span[</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">&#x27;end&#x27;</span><span style="color:#9B60AA">]]}</span><span style="color:#CA492D">&quot;</span><span>)</span></code></pre></div><h2 class="text-web3-20 lg:text-web3-24 font-body">Last thoughts</h2><p class="text-web3-16 font-body">Try Rerank 5 inside a full retrieval stack. <a href="http://www.cohere.com/blog/compass-cloud-beta" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-inherit underline underline-offset-[0.125em] hover:decoration-dashed focus:rounded-[1px] focus:outline focus:outline-1 focus:outline-coral-500 active:decoration-solid active:outline-0 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15 text-neutral-15 visited:text-neutral-15 active:text-neutral-15" style="text-decoration-thickness:0.05em" target="_blank"><span class="whitespace-normal">Compass Cloud</span></a>, our managed search and retrieval platform, brings parsing, embedding, hybrid search and Rerank together in one service, with Cohere managing the infrastructure. It&#x27;s now in private beta for teams building retrieval-heavy and agentic applications. <a href="https://cohere.com/compass#compass-form" class="group inline-block cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap a Cohere-developed methodology designed to address limitations in traditional nDCG caused by incomplete relevance judgments (qrel coverage).\n\nThe advantage is particularly pronounced on BRIGHT, a benchmark testing reasoning-intensive retrieval, where identifying relevant documents requires deeper understanding beyond keyword or semantic similarity. Rerank 5 Pro scores 82.2, outperforming Voyage AI Rerank 3 by 5.8 points and Rerank 4 Pro by 12.4 points, its largest generational gain across the four headline benchmarks. Pro also leads the strongest external competitor by 1.6 points on ViDoRe V3, 1.8 on BEIR, and 3.4 on nanoMTEB. These gains are increasingly important for agentic workflows, where complex, multi-step queries demand the ability to reason about relevance and return evidence that may not closely resemble the original query.\n\nRerank 5 Fast delivers substantial quality gains in the speed-optimized tier. Yet, often only a few sentences within each document are relevant to the query. Processing unnecessary content drives up token costs, increases latency and can introduce noise that reduces answer quality.\\n\\nContext Focus, a new optional capability launching with Rerank 5, goes beyond identifying relevant documents to pinpoint the specific passages that matter most. Configurable thresholds let developers balance context reduction with evidence retention, while a single Rerank API call returns both document-level relevance scores and scored text spans. Applications can then pass only the most relevant evidence to an LLM, without adding steps to the retrieval pipeline.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"af3568f91544\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"142f74ba4127\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Decouple context from answer quality\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"h6\"},{\"_key\":\"00ab49cb5154\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"1b249216745d\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"We measured how answer quality varied as Context Focus removes more of the retrieved text across 30 question-answering datasets. Removing half the context sacrificed only 2 percentage points of answer quality (96% retained), and removing three-quarters lost just 7 (87% answer quality retained). Context Focus therefore works like a dial: moderate settings are close to free, and teams that need bigger savings can trade some quality for up to 10x less context.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"549eb5394360\",\"_type\":\"image\",\"asset\":{\"_ref\":\"image-c4824f5b560c16f076719e632bdd508f35076982-3140x1752-png\",\"_type\":\"reference\"},\"horizontalAlignment\":\"items-center\",\"portableCaption\":[{\"_key\":\"b6320f408544\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"338241200d5e\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Answer quality as retrieved text is removed. Adding Rerank 5 Pro raised accuracy from 46% to 49%. Turning on Context Focus then cut tokens and raised accuracy further. At the default threshold (0.1), tokens per task fell by 19% and accuracy rose to 57%. At 0.4, tokens fell by 34%, lower on every dataset, and accuracy reached 58%. Altogether, the full stack with Context Focus at 0.4 used 35% fewer tokens than BM25 alone, at higher accuracy (58% vs 46%). On the six deep-search benchmarks, it used 41% fewer tokens, and accuracy rose from 39% to 52%, with every benchmark improving on both measures. ​\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"bfc252ad1076\",\"_type\":\"image\",\"asset\":{\"_ref\":\"image-d8f99537c9da84dd6f1417c195908d82bf65a056-3140x1752-png\",\"_type\":\"reference\"},\"horizontalAlignment\":\"items-center\",\"portableCaption\":[{\"_key\":\"9641db563e81\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"486cf27cf701\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Search-agent results, averaged over 17 datasets including six deep-search benchmarks (BrowseComp-Plus, MedBrowseComp, WebWalkerQA, SealQA, HERB and DRBench), with 50 questions per dataset. We follow the evaluation setup of BrowseComp-Plus. The agent is glm-5-3-flash, with search as its only tool and 5 results per search. Retrieval starts with BM25, and Rerank 5 Pro reorders the top 100 with Context Focus switched off. Context Focus is then shown at the default threshold (0.1) and at 0.4, each applied on top of Rerank 5 Pro. Accuracy is the percentage of questions answered correctly. Tokens are the average total per task, summed over every LLM call in the run: input, output and reasoning tokens. The prompt for each call includes the full conversation so far, and cached tokens are included.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"}],\"size\":\"lg\"},{\"_key\":\"7cb8bae95d16\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"36925925c073\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Calibrated relevance scores\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"h5\"},{\"_key\":\"3bdb110e9193\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"9f064a708805\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"With Rerank 5, a score of 0.7 now means a 70% chance the document is useful.\\n\\nEvery reranker returns a relevance score, but until now those scores were mostly good for sorting. On both, Rerank 5 follows the diagonal far more closely than any other reranker. It was an amazing experience.”\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"81fa258f1824\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"78a5befb3e16\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"2003e2416700\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"4bf78e108f28\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"This project, while grounded in cultural and semantic nuance, provided him with more insight into research and a possible future within it. “It gave me a nice sneak peek into what actual researchers are doing,” Gagnier says. “I want to continue research in this field. On BigMathDigits, CALIBER’s ECE of 0.022 is only slightly better than CoCA-lite’s 0.023. But its AUROC is 0.797, compared with 0.508 for CoCA-lite: a large difference in how well confidence distinguishes correct from incorrect answers. Other baselines achieve slightly higher AUROC than CALIBER, so the benefit depends on which property we measure.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"b9d4dee6ab04\",\"_type\":\"image\",\"asset\":{\"_ref\":\"image-7eaad1c3573c53c32606e0a1a0d77478bbb7056e-785x408-svg\",\"_type\":\"reference\"},\"horizontalAlignment\":\"items-center\",\"portableCaption\":[{\"_key\":\"6f36708dceeb\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"62c798101f3a\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Confidence versus observed accuracy on BigMathDigits for the 30B model. Curves closer to the diagonal are better calibrated.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"}],\"size\":\"lg\"},{\"_key\":\"44d1e03020b5\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"0ff378b80609\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"What a change in confidence tells us\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"h5\"},{\"_key\":\"620645b8c975\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"a2c433a9f089\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Having two estimates lets us see whether the model revises its assessment after reasoning. On BigMathDigits, the 7B model’s mean confidence rises from 78.5% to 83.5% for correct answers and falls from 62.6% to 54.5% for incorrect answers. On other datasets, it sometimes lowers confidence for both groups, with a larger decrease for incorrect answers.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"78787d19cb04\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"1b41212d82c8\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"7a2587d5516b\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"e6642bccadcd\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"A rise or fall is therefore context, not a verdict. Two answers that finish at 70% confidence express the same estimated probability of correctness, even if one estimate rose and the other fell. The final estimate assesses the answer; the change tells us how the model’s assessment evolved.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"7735ebc2e571\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"2aec624ef674\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"4df0be4e236f\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"7aae7f8b72c1\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Our experiments take this measurement before and after a single reasoning process. A next question is whether reliable confidence updates could help a model decide when to verify, retrieve more information, or abstain. We have not tested those uses here. CALIBER provides a starting point: confidence trained to reflect what the model can assess at each stage of reasoning.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"1d012b7e2b79\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"c6770687ba6e\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"942b5b1e293a\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"2ccd237810e8\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Read
